Let's consider binary/real sequences with low "central sumset" counts. For m points (mass distributed), central ordered pair sums count should be <m^2/n to get R<2. Let I0(y)=y.
I1(y)=a ∫_0^y [sin2πx + sin2π(y-x)] dx. Linear L(y).
I2(y)=a^2 ∫_0^y sin2πx sin2π(y-x) dx.
Compute I1. Let S1=∫0^y sin2πx dx = (1-cos2πy)/(2π).
S2=∫0^y sin2π(y-x) dx, let t=y-x from y to0? =∫0^y sin2πt dt = same S1. So I1=2a S1 = a/π (1-cos2πy).
I2: use product-to-sum: 1/2[cos(2π(2y-2x? Wait sinA sinB = (cos(A-B)-cos(A+B))/2), A=2πx, B=2π(y-x). A-B=4πx-2πy; A+B=2πy. I2=1/2∫ [cos(4πx-2πy)-cos2πy] dx from0 to y = (1/4π)∫[sin(4πx-2πy)?] - (y cos2πy)/2.
Integral cos(4πx-2πy) dx = [1/(4π) sin(4πx-2πy)]_0^y = (1/(4π))(sin(2πy)-sin(-2πy)) = (2 sin2πy)/(4π)= sin2πy/(2π). Times 1/2 -> sin2πy/(4π). So I2= sin2πy/(4π) - y cos2πy/2.
Thus for y∈[0,1]:

Let's design a multi-stage algorithm in code to maximize chance:
1. Generate a pool of candidate sequences of length n0 (e.g., 128) from:
   - Exponential decreasing/increasing with various λ.
   - Sine-cosine perturbations of exponential/uniform with random Fourier coefficients (smooth).
   - Random softmax smooth: z_i = sum random low-frequency modes.
   - Downsample/upsample of height_sequence_1 if available (to length n0) to get diverse starts.
   - Determine best by exact score, maybe a few random restarts.
2. Run a generic local optimizer (L-BFGS with LSE) on top few candidates, with increasing beta. Keep best exact sequence.
3. If time remains, try optimizing at larger lengths (e.g., 256) by upsampling (linear interpolation) the best candidate or using fresh random, and refine.
4. If time remains, perform deterministic/incremental block local search (multiplicative perturbations) on best sequence to squeeze.
5. Return best list. Call evaluate_sequence at end to print but not necessary; ensure if our score_array maybe different due clipping? Use our score no clipping (values positive). For final list, use float.

Let's define helper `compute_score_and_grad_smooth(z, beta, n=None)`.
- n = z.size.
- zmax subtract for softmax.
- p = np.exp(z-zmax); p/=p.sum()
- C = np.convolve(p,p)
- logsumexp: scale=beta*C; m=scale.max(); e=np.exp(scale-m); s=e.sum(); w=e/s; lse = (m + np.log(s))/beta; f=2*n*lse
- Gradient: q loop or FFT. Let's implement vectorized sliding dot via FFT for speed and elegance.

Let's design block optimizer:
- Input n (full length), B (number blocks, divisor), L=n//B.
- `block_fun(zb, beta)`:
  - q = softmax(zb) length B.
  - p = np.repeat(q / L, L) length n (assuming n divisible).
  - compute C, LSE, G_p as before (4n qdot).
  - H block: for each block b, H_b = (1/L) sum G over indices. Can compute by reshape G (B,L).mean(axis=1).
  - grad_zb = q*(H - dot(q,H)).
  - return f, grad.
- SciPy minimize on zb. Very fast (B=64, n=512 C O(n^2) 262k per eval, but fewer). Could run thousands.

`_score_array` exact:
```
def _score_array(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return inf
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)  # no need but mirror
    if not np.isfinite(arr).all(): return inf
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return inf
    arr=arr/s
    c=np.convolve(arr, arr)
    m=c.max()
    return float(2.0*arr.size*max(0.0,m))
```
Note original sum is of original, but scale invariant; normalize. If arr all zero s=0. If arr has negative clipped to0; our generated nonneg.

```
def _conv_grad_weighted(p, w):
    n=p.size
    try:
       return np.lib.stride_tricks.sliding_window_view(w, n) @ p
    except Exception:
       out=np.empty(n,float)
       for i in range(n): out[i]=np.dot(p,w[i:i+n])
       return out
```

Now, random Fourier starts:
```
def _random_block_z(B, rng, mode_max=6, smooth_scale=1.0, base=None):
    bc = (np.arange(B)+0.5)/B
    z=np.zeros(B)
    if base is not None: z=base.copy()
    else: z += smooth_scale * rng.normal(0,1.0,size=B)
    for m in range(1, mode_max+1):
        amp = 1.0/(m+1)*smooth_scale
        z += amp*(rng.normal(0,1)*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*bc) + rng.normal(0,1)*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*bc))
    return z
```
For smooth, maybe high modes less. Also add linear trend: `rng.normal(0,1)*np.linspace(-1,1,B)`.

```
def _resample(a, n):
   if a.size==n: return a.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
   b=np.clip(b, 1e-15, None); b/=b.sum(); return b
```

Now, final polish:
```
def _polish_sequence(a, rng, deadline):
   a=np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
   a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12 return None; a=a/s
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_array(best)
   no=0; sigma=0.15; it=0
   n=a.size
   min_width=max(2, n//64); max_width=max(min_width+1, n//8)
   while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < 20000:
      cand=best.copy()
      for _ in range(2):
         w=int(rng.integers(min_width, max_width+1)); st=int(rng.integers(0, n-w+1)); cand[st:st+w]*=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma)))
      cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); cs=cand.sum(); if cs>1e-12: cand/=cs
      sc=_score_array(cand)
      if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
         best=cand; best_sc=sc; no=0; sigma=max(0.05, sigma*0.95)
      else:
         no+=1
         if no % 300 == 0:
            sigma=min(0.6, sigma*1.25) if no<1000 else max(0.05, sigma*0.9)
      it+=1
   return best, best_sc
```

```
def _run_block_search(n, B, L, rng, max_starts=30, max_optim=8, deadline=0.0):
    scored=[]
    # generate starts
    for z in _generate_block_starts(n,B,L,rng, maybe seeded):

       if finite: scored.append((sc,z.copy()))
    sorted(scored)
    best_p=None; best_sc=inf
    betas=[n//4,n//2,n,3*n]
    maxfuns=[400,300,200,100]
    for sc,z in scored[:max_optim]:
       if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
       p,sc = _optimize_block(z,B,L,betas,maxfuns,deadline)
       if sc<best_sc: best_p=p; best_sc=sc
    if best_p is None and scored: best_p, best_sc=...
    return best_p,best_sc
```

`_optimize_block` loops stages calls minimize. It returns best exact across stages (not necessarily final low beta? track). For each stage, if fun raises due deadline, use last z. If res status not zero, still use res.x if finite.
```
def _optimize_block(z, B,L,betas, maxfuns, deadline):
   best_p=_block_sequence(z,B,L); best_sc=_score_array(best_p)
   for beta, mf in zip(...):
      if time>=deadline: break
      try:
        res=minimize(_block_lseq_fun, z, args=(beta,B,L), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=False, options={'maxiter': mf, 'maxfun': mf*3, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxls':20})
        if res.x finite: z=res.x
      except Exception: pass
      p=_block_sequence(z,B,L); sc=_score_array(p); update
   return p,best_sc
```

One more important point: `np.convolve` for p sum1 with length 512 direct O(n^2) might be slower than FFT. For many evals, maybe 2B ops. Could use FFT to compute convolution for scoring and LSE? Since functions need C. We can implement a fast convolution using FFT to speed for n=512/1024. `np.fft.rfft` length power2 at least 2n-1. C = irfft(fft(p)^2, N)[:2n-1]. Because p real. This is O(n log n), faster for repeated evals. However, direct convolve might use highly optimized C but O(n^2); for n=512, 262k; FFT ~512 log 1024 ~5k, faster. For n=256 similar. Using FFT in objective may speed significantly. But caution: Numerical errors can produce tiny negative C due to roundoff; clip to0. Use real. Let's implement `_conv_sq(a)`:
```
def _conv_sq(a):
   n=a.size
   m=2*n-1
   N=1
   while N<m: N <<=1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:m]
   return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
This has overhead of FFT each call. For 256, N=512, FFT maybe fast. Direct np.convolve may be faster for small n? For many evals, FFT likely. We can use it for n>=128. But FFT allocates and uses complex; okay. For small n=64, maybe direct. We can branch: if n>=128 use FFT, else direct. Need ensure objective gradient weights w correspond to C positive. With FFT, C length exact maybe small imaginary discarded. Errors ~1e-16, clip.

Using FFT changes score slightly but close. Evaluator uses direct; our final best may differ by tiny. Fine.

Let's implement:
```
def _conv_sq(a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    if n < 64:
       c = np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
       m = 2*n - 1
       N = 1 << (m-1).bit_length() # if m power? `int(np.ceil(m/256)*256)?` Use next power.
       fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
       c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:m]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    c = np.where(c > 1e-15, c, 0.0)
    return c
```
`(m-1).bit_length()` gives next power? Example m=255, (254).bit_length=8, 1<<8=256 OK. m=257, (256).bit_length=9, 512. Good. Or `N=1; while N<m: N*=2`.

Now, `_score_array` uses normalized p and `_conv_sq`. Since p sum maybe 1. Good.

If `minimize is None`, we can call `_simple_gd_full` maybe. We'll implement a fallback `_simple_gd(func, z0, beta, max_iter, deadline)`.

Simple GD:
```
def _simple_gd(func, z0, beta, max_iter, deadline):
   z=z0.copy(); lr=0.1; f,g=func(z,beta)
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if deadline... break
      if not finite f: break
      gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if gn==0 or gn<1e-10: break
      step=lr
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(10):
         zt=z - step*g
         ft,gt=func(zt,beta)
         if finite ft and ft < f: accept; break
         step*=0.5
      if not accept:
         lr*=0.5
         if lr<1e-8: break
      else:
         z=zt; f=ft
   return z
```
But func for block has additional args B,L; can't reuse easily. We can define wrapper? closures not allowed. Could skip GD; assume scipy. Fallback can just return initial.

Since scipy allowed, likely available.

Initial block starts:
```
def _generate_block_starts(n,B,L,rng,seed_seq=None, num_random=40):
   starts=[]
   bc=(np.arange(B)+0.5)/B
   starts.append(np.zeros(B))
   for lam in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0,1.3,1.8,2.5]:
      starts.append(-lam*bc)
      starts.append(lam*(1.0-bc))
   for _ in range(15):
      w=rng.uniform(0.05,0.95); a=rng.uniform(0.2,5.0); b=rng.uniform(0.2,5.0)
      p = w*np.exp(-a*bc) + (1-w)*np.exp(-b*(1.0-bc))
      z=np.log(p+1e-15)
      z += rng.normal(0.0,0.2,size=B)
      starts.append(z)
   # random smooth
   for _ in range(num_random):
      z=np.zeros(B)
      z += rng.normal(0.0,0.25,size=B)
      for m in range(1,6):
         amp=1.0/(m+1.0)
         z += amp*(rng.normal()*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*bc)+rng.normal()*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*bc))
      starts.append(z)
   if seed_seq is not None:
      z=_resample_to_block_z(seed_seq,B)
      if z is not None: starts.append(z)
   return starts
```

Detailed code snippets:

```
_GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0

def _softmax(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-1000.0)
    m = float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(v - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        e = np.zeros_like(v); e[0]=1.0; return e
    return e / s
```

`_conv_sq`:
```
def _conv_sq(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n < 64:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        m=2*n-1
        N=1
        while N < m:
            N <<= 1
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:m]
    c=np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c=np.where(c > 1e-15, c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Potential if c has negative due to very small; clip. If sum p=1, max c maybe. Good.

`_score_array`:
```
def _score_array(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size ==0: return np.inf
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-12: return np.inf
    arr=arr/s
    c=_conv_sq(arr)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0: m=0.0
    return float(2.0*arr.size*m)
```
Note original evaluates with sum of clipped arr; we normalize. Scale invariant. If original arr values >1000 clipped; our arr max maybe. Since p sum1, max<1. Fine.

`_shift_dot`:
```
def _shift_dot(p,w):
    n=p.size
    try:
        win=np.lib.stride_tricks.sliding_window_view(w, n)
        return win @ p
    except Exception:
        out=np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            out[i]=np.dot(p, w[i:i+n])
        return out
```
If `w` length <n? In our use len(w)=2n-1, okay. If not, return zeros? add check:
```
if w.size < n: return np.zeros(n)
```

`_full_lseq_fun`:
```
def _full_lseq_fun(z, beta):
    if _GLOBAL_DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        raise RuntimeError('deadline')
    z=np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=z.size
    if n ==0: return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=float)
    beta=max(1.0, float(beta))
    p=_softmax(z)
    c=_conv_sq(p)
    scale=beta*c
    mx=float(np.max(scale))
    e=np.exp(scale-mx)
    s=float(e.sum())
    w=e/s
    lse=(mx + np.log(s))/beta
    f=2.0*float(n)*lse
    qdot=_shift_dot(p,w)
    G=4.0*float(n)*qdot
    g=p*(G - float(np.dot(p,G)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): g=np.zeros_like(z)
    return float(f), g
```
If c all zeros (no), log(s) finite. If `s==0` due exp underflow? scale-mx ≤0, exp(0)=1 for max, s>=1. Good.

`_block_lseq_fun`:
```
def _block_lseq_fun(z, beta, B, L):
   deadline check
   B=int(B); L=int(L); n=B*L
   z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel(); if size!=B? maybe resize? If wrong, return inf zeros length B? but z size B.
   q=_softmax(z)
   p=np.repeat(q / float(L), L)
   c=_conv_sq(p)
   ... G=4n*qdot
   H=G.reshape(B, L).mean(axis=1)
   g=q*(H - float(np.dot(q,H)))
   return f,g
```
`p` length n; sum = L*∑q/L=1. Good.

```
def _optimize_full(z, betas, maxfuns, deadline):
    z=np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_p=_full_sequence(z); best_sc=_score_array(best_p)
    for beta,mf in zip(betas, maxfuns):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       if minimize is None: break
       options={'maxiter': int(mf), 'maxfun': int(max(10, mf*4)), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxls':20}
       try:
          res=minimize(_full_lseq_fun, z, args=(float(beta),), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=False, options=options)
          if res is not None and hasattr(res, 'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
              z=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
       except Exception:
          pass
       p=_full_sequence(z)
       sc=_score_array(p)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_p=p.copy()
    return best_p, best_sc
```

```
def _simple_gradient(z, func, args, max_iter, deadline):
    z=np.array(z,float); lr=0.2
    f,g=func(z,*args)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time... break
       if not finite f: break
       gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gn<1e-10: break
       step=lr
       for _ in range(8):
          zt=z-step*g
          try: ft,gt=func(zt,*args)
          except: break
          if finite ft and ft < f:
             z=zt; f=ft; g=gt; accepted=True; break
          step*=0.5
       if not accepted:
          lr*=0.5
          if lr<1e-8: break
    return z
```
Then in optimize if minimize None, call `_simple_gradient` with same args for each beta. This is not very strong but can improve. Good.

`_resample_to_block_z(seq, B)`:
```
def _resample_to_block_z(seq, B):
    a=np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return None
    a=a/s
    edges=np.linspace(0, a.size, B+1).astype(np.int64)
    vals=np.empty(B, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(B):
        sl=a[edges[i]:edges[i+1]]
        if sl.size==0: vals[i]=1e-12
        else: vals[i]=float(np.mean(sl))
    vals=np.clip(vals,1e-15,None)
    return np.log(vals)
```

`_resample_seq(a,n)`:
```
def _resample_seq(a,n):
    arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr,0.0),0.0,None)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<1e-12 or n<=0: return None
    arr=arr/s
    if arr.size == n:
      return arr.copy()
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    b=np.interp(x1,x0,arr)
    b=np.clip(b,1e-15,None)
    b=b/b.sum()
    return b
```

```
def _quick_simple_candidates(n, rng, trials=80):
    u=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    cands=[]
    cands.append(np.ones(n))
    for lam in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0]:
       cands.append(np.exp(-lam*u))
       cands.append(np.exp(lam*(1.0-u)))
    for a in [0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0]:
       cands.append((u+1e-8)**a)
       cands.append((1.0-u+1e-8)**a)
    for _ in range(trials):
       w=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.95))
       a=float(rng.uniform(0.2,4.0))
       b=float(rng.uniform(0.2,4.0))
       p = w*np.exp(-a*u) + (1.0-w)*np.exp(-b*(1.0-u))
       if rng.random()<0.3:
          p = p * (1.0 + 0.3*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.uniform(1,4)*u + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)))
       p=np.clip(p,0.0,None)
       if p.sum()>0: cands.append(p)
    best=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for p in cands:
       p=np.asarray(p,float); p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<1e-12: continue
       p=p/s; sc=_score_array(p)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=p.copy()
    return best, best_sc
```
If `p` has all zeros (sin negative could make all zero if amplitude >1?), use max. Use `p=np.maximum(p,0)`. Good.

```
def _optimize_block(z, B, L, betas, maxfuns, deadline):
    z=np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_p=_block_sequence(z,B,L); best_sc=_score_array(best_p)
    for beta,mf in zip(betas,maxfuns):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       args=(float(beta), int(B), int(L))
       if minimize is not None:
          options={'maxiter': int(mf), 'maxfun': int(max(20, mf*4)), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxls':20}
          try:
             res=minimize(_block_lseq_fun, z, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=False, options=options)
             if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                 z=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
          except Exception:
             pass
       else:
          try: z=_simple_gradient(z, _block_lseq_fun, args, int(mf), deadline)
          except Exception: pass
       p=_block_sequence(z,B,L); sc=_score_array(p); if finite update
    return best_p,best_sc
```

```python
"""
Search for a low-peak self-convolution nonnegative step sequence.

Algorithm summary
-----------------
The objective is scale-invariant.  We work with normalized probability
sequences p >= 0, sum(p)=1, and minimize

    2*n*max_k (p*p)_k,

where (p*p)_k is the full convolution coefficient.

The main search combines:
  * many random smooth/low-dimensional block starts,
  * local optimization of block-parameter densities via L-BFGS on a
    log-sum-exp smoothed max-convolution loss,
  * refinement of the best block solution on the full sequence,
  * an optional larger-length refinement,
  * final exact-score local polishing.

All final values are non-negative floats and finite.
"""

import math
import time

import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

_GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0


def _softmax(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-700.0)
    m = float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(v - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        e = np.zeros_like(v, dtype=np.float64)
        e[0] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    return e / s


def _conv_sq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n < 64:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        m = 2 * n - 1
        N = 1
        while N < m:
            N <<= 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:m]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(c > 1e-15, c, 0.0)
    return c


def _score_array(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    arr = arr / s
    c = _conv_sq(arr)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * arr.size * m)


def _shift_dot(p, w):
    n = p.size
    if w.size < n:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        win = np.lib.stride_tricks.sliding_window_view(w, n)
        return np.asarray(win @ p, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            out[i] = float(np.dot(p, w[i:i + n]))
        return out


def _full_sequence_from_z(z):
    return _softmax(z)


def _block_sequence_from_z(z, B, L):
    B = int(B)
    L = int(L)
    q = _softmax(z)
    if q.size != B:
        q = _softmax(np.resize(q, B))
    return np.repeat(q / float(max(1, L)), L)


def _full_lseq_fun(z, beta):
    if _GLOBAL_DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        raise RuntimeError("deadline")
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    beta = max(1.0, float(beta))

    p = _softmax(z)
    c = _conv_sq(p)
    scale = beta * c
    mx = float(np.max(scale))
    e = np.exp(scale - mx)
    s = float(e.sum())
    w = e / s
    lse = (mx + math.log(max(s, 1e-300))) / beta
    f = 2.0 * float(n) * lse

    qdot = _shift_dot(p, w)
    G = 4.0 * float(n) * qdot
    g = p * (G - float(np.dot(p, G)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(f), g


def _block_lseq_fun(z, beta, B, L):
    if _GLOBAL_DEADLINE > 0.0 and time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        raise RuntimeError("deadline")
    B = int(B)
    L = int(L)
    n = B * L
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size != B:
        z = np.resize(z, B)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = max(1.0, float(beta))
    q = _softmax(z)
    p = np.repeat(q / float(max(1, L)), L)

    c = _conv_sq(p)
    scale = beta * c
    mx = float(np.max(scale))
    e = np.exp(scale - mx)
    s = float(e.sum())
    w = e / s
    lse = (mx + math.log(max(s, 1e-300))) / beta
    f = 2.0 * float(n) * lse

    qdot = _shift_dot(p, w)
    G = 4.0 * float(n) * qdot
    H = G.reshape(B, max(1, L)).mean(axis=1)
    g = q * (H - float(np.dot(q, H)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(f), g


def _simple_gradient(z, func, args, max_iter, deadline):
    z = np.array(z, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    lr = 0.2
    try:
        f, g = func(z, *args)
    except Exception:
        return z
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        drift = np.random.default_rng(123).normal(0.0, 0.1, size=z.size)
        z = z + drift
    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        try:
            if not np.isfinite(f) or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                break
        except Exception:
            break
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gn) or gn < 1e-12:
            break
        accepted = False
        step = lr
        for _ls in range(10):
            zt = z - step * g
            try:
                ft, gt = func(zt, *args)
            except Exception:
                break
            if np.isfinite(ft) and ft < f:
                z = zt
                f = ft
                g = gt
                accepted = True
                break
            step *= 0.5
        if not accepted:
            lr *= 0.5
            if lr < 1e-9:
                break
    return z


def _optimize_full(z, betas, maxfuns, deadline):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = _full_sequence_from_z(z)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_array(best_p)

    for beta, mf in zip(betas, maxfuns):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        args = (float(beta),)
        if _scipy_minimize is not None:
            options = {
                "maxiter": int(mf),
                "maxfun": int(max(20, mf * 4)),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxls": 20,
            }
            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(
                    _full_lseq_fun,
                    z,
                    args=args,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=False,
                    options=options,
                )
                if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                    z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            try:
                z = _simple_gradient(z, _full_lseq_fun, args, int(mf), deadline)
            except Exception:
                pass

        p = _full_sequence_from_z(z)
        sc = _score_array(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = p.copy()

    return best_p, best_sc


def _optimize_block(z, B, L, betas, maxfuns, deadline):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = _block_sequence_from_z(z, B, L)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_array(best_p)

    for beta, mf in zip(betas, maxfuns):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        args = (float(beta), int(B), int(L))
        if _scipy_minimize is not None:
            options = {
                "maxiter": int(mf),
                "maxfun": int(max(20, mf * 4)),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxls": 20,
            }
            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(
                    _block_lseq_fun,
                    z,
                    args=args,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=False,
                    options=options,
                )
                if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                    z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            try:
                z = _simple_gradient(z, _block_lseq_fun, args, int(mf), deadline)
            except Exception:
                pass

        p = _block_sequence_from_z(z, B, L)
        sc = _score_array(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = p.copy()

    return best_p, best_sc


def _quick_simple_candidates(n, rng, trials=80):
    n = int(n)
    u = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    cands = []

    cands.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    for lam in (0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        cands.append(np.exp(-lam * u))
        cands.append(np.exp(lam * (1.0 - u)))

    for a in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
        cands.append((u + 1e-8) ** a)
        cands.append((1.0 - u + 1e-8) ** a)

    for _ in range(int(trials)):
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95))
        a = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 4.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 4.0))
        p = w * np.exp(-a * u) + (1.0 - w) * np.exp(-b * (1.0 - u))
        if rng.random() < 0.35:
            freq = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 4.0))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            p = p * (1.0 + 0.30 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * u + phase))
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
        if p.sum() > 1e-15:
            cands.append(p)

    best_p = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    for p in cands:
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s < 1e-15:
            continue
        p = p / s
        sc = _score_array(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = p.copy()
    return best_p, best_sc


def _resample_tuple(a, n):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    n = int(n)
    if s < 1e-15 or n <= 0:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    if arr.size == n:
        return arr.copy()
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(x1, x0, arr)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.clip(b, 1e-15, None)
    b = b / float(b.sum())
    return b


def _resample_to_block_z(seq, B):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    B = int(B)
    if s < 1e-15 or B <= 0:
        return None
    a = a / s
    edges = np.linspace(0, a.size, B + 1).astype(np.int64)
    vals = np.empty(B, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(B):
        sl = a[edges[i]:edges[i + 1]]
        if sl.size == 0:
            vals[i] = 1e-15
        else:
            vals[i] = float(np.mean(sl))
    vals = np.clip(vals, 1e-15, None)
    return np.log(vals)


def _generate_block_starts(n, B, L, rng, seed_seq=None, num_random=40):
    B = int(B)
    bc = (np.arange(B, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(max(1, B))
    starts = []

    starts.append(np.zeros(B, dtype=np.float64))

    for lam in (0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.3, 1.8, 2.5):
        starts.append(-lam * bc)
        starts.append(lam * (1.0 - bc))

    for _ in range(18):
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95))
        a = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 5.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 5.0))
        p = w * np.exp(-a * bc) + (1.0 - w) * np.exp(-b * (1.0 - bc))
        p = np.clip(p, 1e-15, None)
        z = np.log(p) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.2, size=B)
        starts.append(z)

    for _ in range(20):
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        l1 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 4.5))
        l2 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 4.5))
        p1 = np.exp(-l1 * bc)
        p2 = np.exp(-l1 * c) * np.exp(-l2 * (bc - c))
        p = np.where(bc < c, p1, p2) + 1e-12
        z = np.log(p) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, size=B)
        starts.append(z)

    for _ in range(10):
        a = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(-2.5, 2.5))
        z = a * bc + b * bc * bc
        z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, size=B)
        starts.append(z)

    for _ in range(max(1, int(num_random))):
        z = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=B)
        for m in range(1, 6):
            amp = 1.0 / float(m + 1)
            z += amp * (
                float(rng.normal()) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * bc) +
                float(rng.normal()) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * bc)
            )
        starts.append(z)

    if seed_seq is not None:
        z0 = _resample_to_block_z(seed_seq, B)
        if z0 is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(z0)):
            starts.append(z0.copy())
            for _ in range(4):
                starts.append(z0 + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=B))

    return starts


def _run_block_search(n, B, L, rng, max_starts, max_optim, deadline, seed_seq=None):
    starts = _generate_block_starts(n, B, L, rng, seed_seq=seed_seq)
    scored = []
    for idx, z in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        p = _block_sequence_from_z(z, B, L)
        sc = _score_array(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, idx, np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy()))

    if not scored:
        return None, np.inf

    scored.sort()

    best_p = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    betas = [
        max(1, n // 4),
        max(1, n // 2),
        max(1, n),
        max(1, 4 * n),
    ]
    maxfuns = [300, 240, 180, 120]

    for sc, idx, z in scored[:max(1, int(max_optim))]:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        p, sc2 = _optimize_block(z, B, L, betas, maxfuns, deadline)
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = p.copy()

    if best_p is None:
        sc, idx, z = scored[0]
        best_sc = sc
        best_p = _block_sequence_from_z(z, B, L)

    return best_p, best_sc


def _polish_sequence(a, rng, deadline):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15 or a.size < 4:
        return a, np.inf
    a = a / s
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_array(best)
    n = a.size
    min_width = max(2, n // 80)
    max_width = max(min_width + 1, n // 8)

    sigma = 0.18
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    max_iter = 30000

    while time.monotonic() < max(0.0, deadline - 1.0) and it < max_iter:
        it += 1
        cand = best.copy()
        for _ in range(2):
            w = int(rng.integers(min_width, max_width + 1))
            w = min(w, n)
            st = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
            mul = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
            cand[st:st + w] *= mul
        cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
        cs = float(cand.sum())
        if cs < 1e-15:
            continue
        cand = cand / cs
        sc = _score_array(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = cand.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            no_improve = 0
            sigma = max(0.05, sigma * 0.95)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 0 and no_improve % 350 == 0:
                if no_improve < 1400:
                    sigma = min(0.65, sigma * 1.25)
                else:
                    sigma = max(0.05, sigma * 0.90)

    return best, best_sc


def _fallback_sequence(n=128):
    n = int(n)
    u = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    p = np.exp(-u) + 1e-12
    p = p / float(p.sum())
    return p


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    """Return the best nonnegative sequence found within budget_s seconds."""
    global _GLOBAL_DEADLINE

    start_t = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 400.0
    budget = max(1.0, budget - 8.0)
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = start_t + budget

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    parent_arr = None
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
        arr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if arr.size > 0 and arr.sum() > 1e-15:
            parent_arr = arr
            sc = _score_array(arr)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = arr.copy()
                print(f"parent score={sc:.9f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Quick deterministic/random low-dimensional candidates.
    for n in (128, 256):
        if time.monotonic() >= _GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
            break
        p, sc = _quick_simple_candidates(n, rng, trials=120)
        if p is not None and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = p.copy()
        print(f"quick n={n} score={sc:.9f}", flush=True)

    # Main block-parameter search at moderate length.
    if time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 10.0:
        n1 = 256
        B1 = n1 // 4
        L1 = 4
        p, sc = _run_block_search(
            n1, B1, L1, rng,
            max_starts=90,
            max_optim=14,
            deadline=_GLOBAL_DEADLINE,
            seed_seq=parent_arr if parent_arr is not None else best_seq,
        )
        if p is not None and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = p.copy()
        print(f"block n={n1} B={B1} score={sc:.9f}", flush=True)

        # Full-sequence refinement of the best n1 solution.
        if time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 10.0 and best_seq is not None:
            ref = _resample_tuple(best_seq, n1)
            if ref is not None:
                z = np.log(np.clip(ref, 1e-300, None))
                betas = [n1 // 4, n1 // 2, n1, 4 * n1]
                maxfuns = [900, 700, 500, 300]
                p2, sc2 = _optimize_full(z, betas, maxfuns, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE)
                if p2 is not None and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_seq = p2.copy()
            print(f"full refine n={n1} score={sc2:.9f}", flush=True)

    # Larger-length refinement if useful and time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 70.0:
        n2 = 512
        B2 = n2 // 4
        L2 = 4
        p, sc = _run_block_search(
            n2, B2, L2, rng,
            max_starts=40,
            max_optim=6,
            deadline=_GLOBAL_DEADLINE,
            seed_seq=best_seq,
        )
        if p is not None and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = p.copy()
        print(f"block n={n2} B={B2} score={sc:.9f}", flush=True)

        if time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 20.0 and best_seq is not None:
            ref = _resample_tuple(best_seq, n2)
            if ref is not None:
                z = np.log(np.clip(ref, 1e-300, None))
                betas = [n2 // 4, n2 // 2, n2, 4 * n2]
                maxfuns = [700, 500, 350, 220]
                p2, sc2 = _optimize_full(z, betas, maxfuns, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE)
                if p2 is not None and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_seq = p2.copy()
            print(f"full refine n={n2} score={sc2:.9f}", flush=True)

    # Exact local polishing, if the incumbent length is reasonable.
    if time.monotonic() < _GLOBAL_DEADLINE - 4.0 and best_seq is not None:
        if int(np.asarray(best_seq).size) <= 1024:
            p, sc = _polish_sequence(best_seq, rng, time.monotonic() + 0.0)
            if p is not None and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = p.copy()
            print(f"polished score={sc:.9f}", flush=True)
        else:
            small = _resample_tuple(best_seq, 512)
            if small is not None:
                p, sc = _polish_sequence(small, rng, time.monotonic() + 0.0)
                if p is not None and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = p.copy()
                print(f"polished resampled score={sc:.9f}", flush=True)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = _fallback_sequence(128)

    # Normalize for a compact valid output; objective is scale-invariant.
    final_arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(final_arr), final_arr, 0.0)
    final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final_arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        final_arr = _fallback_sequence(128)
    else:
        final_arr = final_arr / s

    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_arr]

    try:
        final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
        print(f"final evaluate_sequence={final_eval:.9f}", flush=True)
        if not np.isfinite(final_eval):
            fb = _fallback_sequence(128)
            final_list = [float(x) for x in fb]
    except Exception:
        pass

    return final_list
```
